Result: 在12个保留条件-视界单元中声明失败呈现剂量单调性。信用门控规划将烧毁想象力减少62%（95%CI 43-81%），成功率持平、碰撞率相当。匹配风险目标下，验证探针从1.00降至0.36/回合（操作域），成功率0.94对0.98。结算校准保持适度的种子一致性运行点，优于原始瞬时门控。在真实Franka上结算在传感和接触噪声下可操作，部署失败循环可在线重新定价，所有1,062次注册消费可从审计日志回放。

Conclusion: DreamLedger证明了预测可靠性可以从瞬时模型信号转变为持久、可审计的部署对象，在多个环境、多个模型架构（DreamerV3/TD-MPC2/V-JEPA 2-AC）上通用，并在真实硬件上验证了结算和审计闭环的有效性。信用门控显著减少无效想象力消费，同时不损失任务成功率。

Abstract: Robots are beginning to act on world-model predictions, yet reliability is still expressed through instantaneous, model-internal signals. DreamLedger instead treats reliability as a persistent deployment object: an execution-settled credit file recording how often consumed predictions are borne out, indexed by operating condition, region, and prediction horizon, and consulted before each use. Each consumed prediction is registered as a claim; attributable outcomes are settled against arriving reality at zero labeling cost, an attribution stage excludes measurement-contaminated outcomes, and a settlement-supervised head complements sparse bins. The resulting credit gates consumption: low-credit predictions shorten the dependent horizon or trigger additional observation; every reliance event remains auditable via dependency tickets and replayable logs. We evaluate DreamLedger in three simulated domains (indoor flight, tabletop manipulation, 2D navigation), via mounts on unmodified DreamerV3, TD-MPC2, and V-JEPA 2-AC, and on a real Franka manipulator. Claim failure is dose-monotone in all 12 held-out condition-horizon cells. Credit-gated planning reduces burned imagination (consumed claims that later fail to redeem) by 62% (95% CI 43-81%) versus blind consumption, with equal success and comparable collision rates. At matched risk targets, persistent books cut verification probes from 1.00 to 0.36/episode in manipulation, at success 0.94 versus 0.98; settlement-grounded calibration retains moderate, seed-consistent operating points unlike raw instantaneous gates. The same trust layer operates across decoder-, latent-, and token-space interfaces, including V-JEPA 2-AC settled on real robot frames. On hardware, settlement remains operational under real sensing and contact noise, a deployment failure loop is re-priced online, and all 1,062 registered spends replay from the audit logs.

</details>


### [102] [Interpreting Control Latents for System Identification via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23887)
*Dingqi Zhang,Ruiqi Zhang,Mark W. Mueller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将闭环自适应策略的潜在表征（latent）解码为四旋翼物理模型的分布（通过条件流匹配），从而实现不解冻策略的在线预测调参和扰动鲁棒性分析。在扰动执行器动力学下，预测调参将位置RMSE降低23%、航向RMSE降低45%；在高斯力扰动下，解码模型集成能准确预测侧向跟踪误差演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的控制潜在表征仅是策略的内部表示，不是可检查、可推演或可被其他控制模块使用的物理模型。直接将潜在映射到物理参数是欠定的，因为多个系统可能产生相似的闭环行为。这限制了固定策略的闭环分析、诊断与进一步改进。

Method: 使用条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）将每个操作潜在编码解码为四旋翼模型的分布。解码出的分布支持两种下游应用：(1) 在线预测式调参高层控制器（配合固定低层策略）；(2) 在指定扰动下的鲁棒性分析。无需修改原策略本身。

Result: 在扰动执行器动力学下，解码模型预测调参将位置跟踪RMSE降低23%，航向RMSE降低45%（相对固定增益）。在高斯力扰动下，解码模型集成能准确预测侧向跟踪误差演化。

Conclusion: 控制潜在表征可以被转换为物理模型集成，用于冻结自适应策略的调参、鲁棒性分析和诊断，拓展了自适应性控制策略的可解释性和闭环分析能力。

Abstract: Latent-conditioned adaptive policies can control robots across changing dynamics, but their learned latents remain internal representations of the policy rather than physical models that can be inspected, rolled out, or used by other control modules. This limits closed-loop analysis, diagnosis, and further improvement of a fixed policy. A direct mapping from latent to physical parameters is also under-specified, because multiple systems can induce similar closed-loop behavior. We therefore decode each operational latent into a distribution of quadrotor models using conditional flow matching. The decoded distribution enables two downstream uses without modifying the policy: online predictive tuning of a high-level controller around the fixed low-level policy, and robustness analysis under specified disturbances. Under perturbed actuator dynamics, decoded-model predictive tuning reduces position tracking RMSE by $23\%$ and heading RMSE by $45\%$ relative to fixed gains. Under Gaussian force disturbances, decoded-model ensembles closely predict the lateral tracking-error evolution. Together, these results show that control latents can be converted into physical model ensembles for tuning, robustness analysis, and diagnosis of frozen adaptive policies.

</details>


### [103] [Safety-aware Model Predictive Path Integral Control with Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23972)
*Yiqi Zhao,Taekyung Kim,Hideki Okamoto,Bardh Hoxha,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 safety-aware-stl-mppi，一种将离散时间信号时序逻辑（STL）公式编码为时变控制障碍函数（CBF），并集成到模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器中的采样型滚动时域规划框架，用于在时间关键且复杂规格下保证安全性的运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人安全感知运动规划中，当任务具有时间关键性和复杂规格约束时，传统方法难以同时兼顾安全与计算效率。需要一种既能表达复杂时序逻辑约束又具低计算成本的方法。

Method: 将离散时间STL公式编码为候选时变CBF，将其集成进MPPI采样型控制器中，利用采样规划的可并行化低计算成本优势，同时借助CBF实现STL约束的满足。

Result: 在四组多样环境和成本设定的火星车规划案例中，与多个MPPI基线对比，方法一致地实现了高安全性和高效率；并在NVIDIA Isaac Lab中完成了四旋翼规划实验。

Conclusion: 该方法在复杂时空逻辑约束下兼顾了安全性与计算效率，验证了将STL-CBF与MPPI结合的可行性，适用于时间关键的机器人运动规划任务。

Abstract: Safety-aware motion planning remains a challenge in robotics, especially when missions are time-critical and are under complex specifications. In this paper, we propose safety-aware-stl-mppi, a computationally efficient sampling-based receding-horizon planning framework designed to promote satisfaction of constraints expressed in Signal Temporal Logic (STL). Our approach encodes discrete-time STL formulas into candidate time-varying control barrier functions (CBF), which are integrated into a model predictive path integral (MPPI) controller. Our method inherits the benefits of low computational cost from an efficiently parallelizable sampling based planner and utilizes CBF for constraints expressed in STL. We compare against several MPPI baselines using four artificial Mars Rover planning case studies with a diverse environment and cost setups, where we show our method consistently achieving high safety and efficiency. We show a quadcopter planning experiment with NVIDIA Isaac Lab.

</details>


### [104] [Sensorless damage-safe grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23983)
*Yusei Shuto,Danilo Vasconcellos Vargas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需力/力矩传感器、仅靠编码器位置和电机力矩信号即可实现无损伤机器人水果采摘的控制方法。控制器通过估计物体压缩应变来闭合夹爪，当估计应变达到用户指定的极限ε时停止，并通过将基于力矩的接触力除以物体刚度下界来保证实际压缩不超过ε。该方法在MuJoCo仿真和3D打印TPU立方体上验证，在保持抓取成功率的同时大幅降低软物体损伤率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人水果采摘需要牢固抓握但不产生瘀伤，然而同一物种内压缩刚度随成熟度变化数倍，固定夹持力无法覆盖全部范围。与其调节力，不如约束变形量，因为变形量是操作者可解释的、与尺寸成比例的损伤限制。

Method: 控制器在每个伺服夹爪上仅利用编码器位置和电机力矩信号，估计接触力并除以物体刚度的下界，得到保守估计的压缩应变。当估计应变达到用户指定极限ε时停止闭合。推导并量化了接触检测的应变下限，表明检测本身的压缩消耗随闭合速度线性增长，速度成为吞吐量-温和度的显式调节旋钮。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真中，使用校准到真实伺服电机的传感器噪声模型，该控制器在中等至坚硬刚度范围内实现≥98%抓取成功率和0%损伤，覆盖全部认证ε范围，而固定力基准方法无法达到。在刚度分级的3D打印TPU立方体上，以约一半的夹持力匹配基准抓取成功率，并将软物体损伤从100%降至40%。

Conclusion: ε作为认证的、随尺寸缩放、操作者可解释的损伤限制，优于人工调优的力阈值，并且可作为学习抓取策略的安全动作参数。该方法的保守性保证使其在宽刚度范围内可靠工作。

Abstract: Robotic fruit harvesting must hold produce securely without bruising it, yet compression stiffness varies several-fold with ripeness within a single species, so no fixed grip force spans the range. Rather than tune force, we bound deformation: a controller closes the gripper until the object's estimated compression strain reaches a user-specified limit $\varepsilon$, using only the encoder position and motor-effort signal on every servo gripper---no tactile or force-torque sensor. Dividing an effort-based contact force by a lower bound on object stiffness makes the stop provably conservative---true compression stays at or below $\varepsilon$---for any $\varepsilon$ above a contact-detection strain floor we identify and quantify: robust detection itself spends compression, linearly in closing speed, making speed an explicit throughput--gentleness knob. Unlike a hand-tuned force threshold, $\varepsilon$ is a certified, size-scaling, operator-interpretable damage limit, and a ready safe-action parameter for learned grasping policies. In MuJoCo simulation over a realistic fruit-stiffness range, under a sensor-noise model calibrated to the real servo, the controller holds $\ge 98\,\%$ grasp at $0\,\%$ damage across all medium-to-firm stiffnesses for the entire certified $\varepsilon$ range, which neither fixed-force baseline attains; on stiffness-graded 3D-printed TPU cubes it matches baseline grasp success at roughly half the grip force and cuts soft-object damage from $100\,\%$ to $40\,\%$.

</details>


### [105] [Bridging Teacher Expectations and Robot Learning via Coupling Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23994)
*Evan Dallas,Sean Dallas,Wing-Yue Geoffrey Louie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的尺度（scale）用于根据人机教学（human-robot teaching）中人类教师与机器人学习者之间的耦合动力学（coupling dynamics）来分类交互，并将该尺度应用于文献子集，以分析耦合动力学及教师心智模型与机器人学习系统真实情况之间的不匹配对教学效果和人类感知的影响。研究由人类学习理论启发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习的发展，人类教学与机器人学习从离线分离走向在线耦合，但耦合的结构、时序和内容如何影响教学效果与用户感知尚不明确，缺乏系统性的分类与分析框架。

Method: 受人类学习理论启发，提出一个新的分类尺度，根据人类教师与机器人学习者之间的耦合动力学对人机教学交互进行分类；将该尺度应用于人机教学文献的一个子集，考察耦合动力学以及教师心智模型与真实机器人学习系统不匹配对教学效果和人类感知的影响。

Result: 提出了耦合动力学的分类尺度，并通过文献分析识别出耦合动力学与心智模型不匹配如何影响教学效果和人类对教学过程感知的规律。

Conclusion: 耦合动力学是理解人机教学有效性的关键维度，心智模型的不匹配会显著影响教学效果和人类感知，该分类尺度为后续人机教学研究提供了分析框架。

Abstract: Human-robot teaching focuses on enabling nontechnical experts to customize robots according to their needs after deployment. With recent advances in machine learning, human-robot teaching is no longer confined to offline learning where the data gathering step from a human teacher is separated from when the robot learns. Instead, more recent approaches for human-robot teaching focus on coupling human teaching with robot learning. This coupling impacts the structure, timing, and content of the teaching and learning interaction. However, it is currently unclear how such coupling dynamics affect humanrobot teaching effectiveness and human perceptions towards the teaching process. Informed by human learning theories, in this paper we propose a new scale for classifying human-robot teaching interactions according to coupling dynamics present between the human teacher and robot learner. We apply this scale to a subset of the human-robot teaching literature to identify how coupling dynamics and human teacher mental model mismatches with the ground truth robot learning system affect teaching effectiveness and human perceptions towards the teaching process

</details>


### [106] [NeurRAFT: Robot Motion Planning via Anchor-Level Flow Matching with Clearance-Aware Preference Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24026)
*Sibo Tian,Chang Liu,Minghui Zheng,Xiao Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NeurRAFT 是一个基于anchor级流匹配和clearance感知偏好调优的端到端神经运动规划框架。它使用紧凑的anchor waypoints并通过Jacobian加权损失训练，推理时两步集成生成anchor后经三次样条插值恢复全分辨率轨迹。通过Direct Preference Optimization将概率质量转移到更大障碍物间隙的轨迹上，将改进直接吸收进规划器参数。实验在仿真中超越SOTA，并在真实嘈杂、部分遮挡深度观测下零样本迁移到Franka机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端神经运动规划器通常在稠密waypoint序列上建模，浪费容量在冗余的局部细节和平滑性上；且从正面示教进行模仿学习无法区分无碰撞与近碰撞轨迹，导致碰撞倾向行为在测试时持续存在。

Method: 使用紧凑anchor waypoints代替稠密序列；Jacobian加权损失考虑每个anchor在任务空间的冲击；推理时两步生成anchor后三次样条插值。用Direct Preference Optimization在训练阶段重塑预训练规划器的分布，将概率质量移向更大障碍物间隙的轨迹，无需推理时后处理或增广。

Result: 实验表明在多个基准上显著超越现有SOTA神经规划器；真实世界实验中在噪声和部分遮挡深度观测下实现了对Franka机器人的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 通过anchor-level表征降低冗余建模并结合DPO直接偏好优化，可以有效解决神经运动规划中的碰撞问题，且能在真实机器人上零样本部署。

Abstract: Recent end-to-end neural motion planners generate trajectories from raw sensor observations, avoiding the privileged geometric models required by classical planners. However, collision-free planning in cluttered environments remains challenging. We present NeurRAFT, a generative planning framework based on anchor-level flow matching and clearance-aware preference tuning. Unlike prior neural planners that model dense waypoint sequences and spend capacity on redundant local details and smoothness, NeurRAFT operates on compact anchor waypoints. We train the planner using a Jacobian-weighted loss that accounts for the task-space impact of each anchor. At inference, the anchors are generated in two integration steps, followed by cubic-spline interpolation to recover a smooth, full-resolution trajectory. Since imitation learning from positive demonstrations cannot distinguish collision-free from near-collision trajectories, collision-prone behaviors persist at test time. Rather than relying on post-hoc corrections, we directly reshape the pretrained planner's distribution toward safer solutions without augmenting inference. Specifically, Direct Preference Optimization shifts probability mass toward trajectories with larger obstacle clearance, with the resulting improvement directly absorbed into the planner parameters. Experiments show substantial improvements over state-of-the-art planners, while real-world experiments demonstrate zero-shot transfer to a Franka robot under noisy and partially occluded depth observations. Video results available at https://neurraft.github.io/.

</details>


### [107] [Design-to-Plan: A Large Language Model-Based Multi-Agent Framework for Manufacturing Process Planning from 3D CAD Models and 2D Engineering Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24039)
*Muhammad Tayyab Khan,Lequn Chen,Wenhe Feng,Seung Ki Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Design-to-Plan，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体框架，用于端到端制造工艺规划。框架通过编排器协调多个专业智能体（3D特征识别、2D图纸分析、2D-3D上下文融合、知识检索、工艺排序、刀具选择、报告生成），将LLM作为推理智能体与确定性模块和知识源交互，实现从设计工件到工艺计划的完整推理链自动化。在300个基准案例上，下游智能体成功率100%，Tool F1分数95.9%-97.6%，冲突分析源检测准确率90%，关键规划任务token使用量减少60%-68%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有制造工艺规划方法聚焦于孤立子任务（如特征识别、图纸解读、刀具选择），难以支持从设计工件到工艺计划的完整推理链。当规划需要同时理解3D CAD模型、2D工程图纸、材料及领域规则时，这一能力缺口尤为关键。

Method: 提出多智能体LLM框架Design-to-Plan：编排器协调七个专业智能体（3D特征识别、2D图纸分析、2D-3D上下文融合、知识检索、工艺排序、刀具选择、报告生成）。采用混合架构——确定性模块和专用智能体从CAD/图纸输入中提取结构化信息，LLM智能体进行上下文感知推理、检索制造规则、解决冲突并生成规划输出。该系统在三个下游ReAct智能体上使用300个基准案例评估，并单独评估了CAD特征识别、图纸分析和2D-3D上下文融合模块。

Result: 并行架构在下游智能体上实现100%成功率，Tool F1分数达95.9%-97.6%，冲突分析中源检测准确率90%，关键规划任务token使用量降低60%-68%。

Conclusion: 结构化的LLM多智能体协调能够弥合设计表示与制造知识之间的鸿沟，实现可扩展、高效且可追溯的设计到规划自动化。该工作展示了LLM作为推理智能体而非独立文本生成器的混合系统设计优势。

Abstract: Manufacturing process planning transforms heterogeneous design information into coherent manufacturing decisions. However, existing approaches focus on isolated subtasks, such as feature recognition, drawing interpretation, or tool selection, and struggle to support the full reasoning chain from design artifacts to process plans. This is critical when planning must interpret 3D CAD models, 2D engineering drawings, materials, and domain-specific rules. To address this gap, this paper presents Design-to-Plan, a large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent framework for end-to-end manufacturing process planning. An orchestrator coordinates specialized agents for 3D feature recognition, 2D drawing analysis, 2D-3D context fusion, knowledge retrieval, process sequencing, tool selection, and report generation. Rather than using LLMs as standalone text generators, the framework deploys them as reasoning agents that interact with deterministic modules and knowledge sources to produce consistent and traceable decisions. In this hybrid design, deterministic modules and specialized agents extract structured information from CAD and drawing inputs, while LLM agents perform context-aware reasoning, retrieve manufacturing rules, resolve conflicts, and generate planning outputs. The framework is evaluated using 300 benchmark cases across three downstream ReAct-enabled agents, plus separate evaluations of CAD feature recognition, drawing analysis, and 2D-3D context fusion. The parallel architecture achieves 100% success across downstream agents, Tool F1 scores of 95.9%-97.6%, 90% source detection accuracy in conflict analysis, and a 60%-68% reduction in token usage for key planning tasks. Results show that structured LLM-based multi-agent coordination can bridge design representations and manufacturing knowledge, enabling scalable, efficient, and traceable design-to-plan automation.

</details>


### [108] [Hierarchical Skill Retrieval for Data-Efficient Adaptation of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24042)
*Haoran Hao,Shahram Najam Syed,Jeff Schneider,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出层级技能检索（HSR）框架，将目标任务分解为技能序列，结合子任务级语言检索与行为特征重排序，通过两阶段预训练与微调实现数据高效的VLA模型适配，在LIBERO基准和真实机器人任务上分别超越最强基线10.3%和21.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在任务演示数据有限时性能下降，而检索方法多基于视觉相似性、状态-动作表示或任务级语言匹配，忽略了长时程操作任务的层级结构——完整任务匹配稀少但可复用技能往往丰富。

Method: HSR框架包含三个部分：(1) 将目标任务分解为候选技能序列，基于语义合理性与技能可靠性评估选择最优分解；(2) 混合检索，结合子任务级语言检索与行为特征重排序，筛选语义相关且行为兼容的演示；(3) 两阶段预训练与微调管线，分离通用技能获取与任务特定适配。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上平均成功率提升10.3%，在多个真实世界机器人操作任务上平均成功率提升21.3%，均超过最强基线。

Conclusion: 结构化技能层级检索能够有效提升数据高效的VLA适配性能，证明了在跨任务演示中检索可复用技能策略的有效性。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models pretrained on large-scale robot datasets provide a strong foundation for robot manipulation, their performance can degrade when adapted to new tasks with limited task-specific demonstrations. Retrieval offers a practical way to reuse existing demonstrations for data-efficient adaptation, but existing methods often rely on visual similarity, state-action representations, or task-level language matching. These approaches may overlook the hierarchical structure of long-horizon manipulation tasks, where complete task matches are rare but reusable skills are often abundant. To address this challenge, we propose Hierarchical Skill Retrieval (HSR), a retrieval framework for data-efficient VLA adaptation. Specifically, HSR first decomposes a target task into candidate skill sequences. It evaluates each plan based on both semantic plausibility and skill reliability estimated from the prior dataset. The selected decomposition is then used for hybrid retrieval. This combines subtask-level language retrieval with behavior-feature reranking to identify demonstrations that are both semantically relevant and compatible with the target task. Finally, we adapt the policy through a two-stage pretraining and finetuning pipeline, which separates general skill acquisition from task-specific adaptation. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark and several real-world robot manipulation tasks show that HSR improves the average success rate by 10.3% and 21.3% over the strongest baseline, respectively. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of structured skill-level retrieval for data-efficient VLA adaptation. Videos and code are available at https://hoar012.github.io/HSR-Project.

</details>


### [109] [SIREN-Bench: Behavior-Driven Generation and Evaluation of Emergency-Vehicle Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24094)
*Yicheng Zhu,Tianmu Zhao,Haoxin Leng,Fan Zuo,Tao Li,Zilin Bian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SIREN，一个行为驱动的 SUMO–CARLA 协同仿真平台，用于生成紧急车辆与民用车辆（EMV–civilian）交互。配套 SIREN-Bench-v1 基准，包含 7 个参数化交互模板、三个行为族和 L1–L3 紧急等级，并通过 3D 检测、轨迹预测和视觉-语言风险理解三个任务验证，揭示行为相关的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估 EMV 与普通车辆的安全关键交互需要同时控制 EMV 特权和民用车辆行为，并需要一致的传感器感知和真值。现有数据集和仿真基准无法直接提供这种组合。

Method: SIREN 耦合 SUMO 的网络级交通演化与行为逻辑，以及 CARLA 的连续车辆控制和同步车载感知；根据激活的行为，交互由 SUMO、CARLA 或两者联合控制。实例化为 SIREN-Bench-v1，含 7 个参数化交互模板、L1–L3 紧急等级和三个行为族，并配套同步传感器观测与模拟器原生标注。

Result: 评估 9 个轨迹预测器、4 个基于 LiDAR 的检测器和 5 个视觉-语言模型，发现行为依赖的失败模式。交通清障交互对检测最难，特权路口穿行对预测最难，无学习型预测器平均超越恒速基线。VLM 在正常交通上显著优于近碰和碰撞事件。

Conclusion: 行为中心的基准测试揭示了现有模型在 EMV 安全关键场景中的系统性薄弱环节，SIREN 可作为自动驾驶与交通安全研究的数据生成和评估平台。

Abstract: Emergency vehicles (EMVs) can reorganize surrounding traffic as civilian vehicles brake, change lanes, or form rescue corridors in response to their passage. Evaluating these safety-critical interactions requires behavior-level control over both EMV privileges and civilian responses, together with consistent sensing and ground truth. Existing datasets and simulation benchmarks do not directly provide this combination. We present \textbf{SIREN}, a behavior-driven SUMO--CARLA co-simulation platform for generating EMV--civilian interactions. SIREN couples SUMO's network-level traffic evolution and behavior logic with CARLA's continuous vehicle control and synchronized onboard sensing; depending on the active behavior, the interaction is controlled by SUMO, CARLA, or jointly. We instantiate the platform as \textbf{SIREN-Bench-v1}, comprising seven parameterized interaction templates across emergency levels L1--L3 and three behavior families, with synchronized sensor observations and simulator-native annotations. We demonstrate the benchmark through three representative tasks: 3D object detection, trajectory prediction, and vision-language risk understanding. Evaluations of nine trajectory predictors, four LiDAR-based detectors, and five vision-language models reveal behavior-dependent failure modes. Traffic-clearance interactions are hardest for detection, privileged intersection traversal is hardest for prediction, and no learned predictor outperforms the constant-velocity reference on average. Vision-language models perform substantially better on normal traffic than on near-miss and collision events. These results demonstrate the value of behavior-centered benchmarking and establish SIREN as an extensible data-generation and evaluation platform for autonomous-driving and transportation safety research.

</details>


### [110] [TrAct: Bridging Robot Control and Visual Prediction with Visual Tracks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24101)
*Zhi Cao,Howard Ji,Kevin Zhang,Kuangzhi Ge,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Huang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TrAct提出以视觉轨迹（visual tracks）作为机器人在控制与预测之间的中间接口，包含VLAT（联合预测动作和轨迹）、TWM（轨迹条件世界模型预测未来视频）、VLAC（视觉语言奖励模型评分）三个组件。在LIBERO-INTEGRAL基准和真实Franka机械臂上，成功率从27%提升到55%（仿真）以及从49%提升到76%（真实），显著优于VLA基线π0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动作（actions）本质上是具身特定的，与图像空间的视觉变化弱对齐，作为机器人世界模型的条件信号效果有限。需要一种与具体肢体无关、能提供密集图像空间指导的表示来连接控制与预测。

Method: TrAct框架包含三个核心组件：(1) VLAT（Vision-Language-Action-and-Track模型）联合生成候选动作及对应的视觉轨迹；(2) TWM（Track-conditioned World Model）以轨迹为条件预测未来视觉结果；(3) VLAC（Vision-Language Action-reward Critic）对预测的未来视频进行评分选择最优轨迹，执行对应的动作。推理时先生成候选动作-轨迹对，再由TWM展开视觉后果，VLAC裁决选出最优。

Result: 在LIBERO-INTEGRAL基准上成功率从27%提升至55%，在真实Franka机械臂操作任务中从49%提升至76%（对比强VLA基线π0.5）。TWM比动作条件世界模型（AWM）持续提升视频预测质量。

Conclusion: 视觉轨迹提供了机器人在控制与视觉预测之间的有效共享接口，能够实现更准确的世界建模和更强的机器人泛化能力。

Abstract: Robot actions are inherently embodiment-specific and only weakly aligned with image-space visual changes, limiting their effectiveness as conditioning signals for robot world models. In contrast, visual tracks provide an embodiment-agnostic representation of how task-relevant points move through a scene, offering dense image-space guidance for accurate and spatially precise future video prediction. Building on this observation, we propose TrAct, a world-model-based robot decision-making framework that uses visual tracks as an intermediate interface between control and prediction. TrAct consists of three components: a Vision-Language-Action-and-Track model (VLAT) that jointly predicts candidate actions and corresponding visual tracks from the current observation and language instruction; a track-conditioned world model (TWM) that predicts future visual outcomes conditioned on the proposed tracks; and a vision-language reward model (VLAC) that scores the predicted outcomes. At inference time, VLAT generates candidate action-track pairs, TWM rolls out their visual consequences, and VLAC selects the track whose predicted outcome best satisfies the instruction; the action paired with the selected track is then executed by the robot. Experiments on the proposed LIBERO-INTEGRAL benchmark and real-world Franka manipulation show that TrAct improves success rates from 27% to 55% in simulation and from 49% to 76% on real-world tasks compared with the strong VLA baseline $π_{0.5}$. Furthermore, TWM consistently improves video prediction quality over the action-conditioned world model (AWM). These results demonstrate that visual tracks provide an effective shared interface between robot control and visual prediction, enabling more accurate world modeling and stronger robot generalization.

</details>


### [111] [Trajectory-Level Continuous Action Representation for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24111)
*Tong Yang,Jingkai Jia,Yuecheng Xu,Xueyao Chen,Chi Zhang,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CAT，一种针对机器人操作的轨迹级连续动作表示框架。将固定实时区间内的动作轨迹编码为连续潜变量token，通过频率感知位置编码建立共享时间坐标系，以解耦动作表示与控制频率/固定时间参数化，并在LIBERO、MimicGen及真实世界长时操作任务上超越VQ和连续动作基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动系统将动作表示与控制频率纠缠或依赖固定时间参数化，导致高采样率下表示冗余，且限制了关键运动建模能力。需要一种独立于控制频率和时间参数化的统一动作表征。

Method: 1) 将固定实时区间内的动作轨迹编码为连续潜变量token序列（而非离散VQ码本）；2) 引入频率感知位置编码建立跨不同控制频率的共享时间坐标系，保证时间一致性；3) 轨迹级正则化稳定潜表示。整体避免表示随时间步密度增长。

Result: 在LIBERO、MimicGen及真实世界长时操作任务上，基于CAT的策略在匹配训练设置下一致优于VQ类及连续动作基线；在不同模型主干和控制频率下均稳定提升成功率。

Conclusion: 轨迹级连续动作建模在变控制率条件下的可扩展机器人操作中具有显著优势，是一种有效的统一动作表示方案。

Abstract: We propose CAT, a trajectory-level continuous action representation framework for robotic manipulation. Existing visuomotor systems often entangle action representation with control frequency or rely on fixed temporal parameterizations. This leads to representational redundancy at high sampling rates and limits the modeling of critical motion. CAT instead encodes action trajectories within a fixed real-time interval into a set of continuous latent tokens. To ensure temporal consistency across varying control frequencies, we further incorporate a frequency-aware positional encoding that establishs a shared temporal coordinate system. Trajectory-level regularization further stabilizes the latent representation. This approach prevents representation growth with timestep density and avoids reliance on predefined temporal parameterizations. Extensive system-level evaluations on LIBERO, MimicGen, and real-world long-horizon manipulation tasks demonstrate that CAT-based policies consistently outperform both competitive VQ-based and continuous visuomotor baselines under matched training settings. Across various model backbones and control frequencies, CAT consistently improves success rates. These results highlight the advantages of trajectory-level continuous action modeling for scalable robotic manipulation across varying control rates.

</details>


### [112] [PonderPounce: A Pretrained MLLM as an Episode Context Engine for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24115)
*Suhwan Choi,Jaeyoon Jung,Sungkyung Kim,Yunsung Lee,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PonderPounce通过复用MLLM的原生因果上下文作为机器人记忆，将System2 MLLM（Ponder）与System1 VLA（Pounce）联合端到端训练，无需专用记忆模块或桥梁预训练，实现高效的记忆依赖型视觉语言动作策略，在RoboMME和RoboCasa-DC基准上取得显著提升。

score: 93


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型通常继承预训练表示但未利用MLLM的上下文推理能力作为情节记忆，而记忆依赖型策略需要专用历史机制。作者旨在通过复用MLLM原生因果上下文来避免构建专用记忆模块。

Method: Ponder（System2 MLLM）在原生因果上下文中累积情节观察、演示和先前认知，生成子目标文本和演示推理供内部使用；Pounce（System1 VLA）直接接收当前观察、指令和本体感觉，通过Ponder-Pounce接口异步接收最新连续认知token及其年龄。两者联合端到端训练，无专用记忆模块或桥梁预训练。优化服务实现78ms认知刷新和25ms动作调用的p50延迟，支持20Hz动作播放。

Result: RoboMME基准：9B模型达60.83%，0.8B模型达50.04%，优于FrameSamp+Modul（44.51%）和当前观测π_0.5（17.93%）；9x数据下达75.54%（对比57.88%）。RoboCasa-DC：12.5%对比最强演示条件基线11.6%，认知状态替换为空状态时降至8.6%。

Conclusion: PonderPounce表明复用MLLM原生因果上下文作为记忆是可行且高效的，无需专用记忆模块即可显著提升记忆依赖型VLA策略性能，同时保持低延迟的实时推理能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can integrate long visual histories, reason under partial observability, and infer behavior from a few examples. Yet vision-language-action (VLA) models generally inherit pretrained representations without using this contextual capacity as episode memory. Memory-dependent policies address this gap through purpose-built history mechanisms. PonderPounce instead reuses an MLLM's native causal context as robot memory. Ponder, a System2 MLLM, accumulates episode observations, demonstrations, and prior cognition in its native causal context and can generate subgoal text and demonstration reasoning for internal use. Pounce, a System1 VLA, receives the current observation, instruction, and proprioception directly; through the Ponder--Pounce interface, it asynchronously receives only the newest continuous cognition token and its age. Both are jointly trained end to end without a purpose-built memory module or separate bridge pretraining. Optimized serving achieves p50 latencies of 78ms for cognition refresh and 25ms for action-model invocation, supporting 20Hz action playback. On RoboMME with base-scale training data, PonderPounce reaches 60.83% with 9B and 50.04% with 0.8B under the same Pounce architecture and interface, versus 44.51% for FrameSamp+Modul and 17.93% for the current-observation π_{0.5}. With 9x data, it reaches 75.54% versus 57.88% for FrameSamp+Modul. On RoboCasa-DC, the same interface learns from action supervision alone and reaches 12.5% versus 11.6% for the strongest published demonstration-conditioned baseline, falling to 8.6% when cognition is replaced by a learned null state.

</details>


### [113] [Coverage Planning for Robotic Tooth Preparation in Densely Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24155)
*Yunwen Li,Chen Chen,Xiangjie Yan,Chang Shu,Jianxia Hou,Shiji Song,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套自主全冠牙体预备的机器人系统，包含解剖感知的刀具路径规划算法和间隙导向的末端执行器偏航分配策略，在仿真与仿真头模实验中实现了0.117 mm的平均几何偏差（RMSE），兼顾修复质量与临床安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙体预备是修复牙科的核心操作，要求亚毫米级精度，但口腔内工作空间高度受限，机器人自动化面临切削工具需在密集约束空间内精确操作的巨大挑战。

Method: 1) 解剖感知的刀具路径规划算法，精确贴合技师设计的预备模型并保护邻牙；2) 间隙导向的末端执行器偏航分配策略，允许口内进入同时降低软组织干涉风险。

Result: 平均几何偏差0.117 mm（RMSE），满足修复质量与临床安全要求；仿真和仿真头模实验验证了系统可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的机器人系统在牙体预备任务上达到了临床可接受的精度和安全性，证明了自动化口腔修复手术的可行性。

Abstract: Tooth preparation refers to the controlled removal of tooth structure to create an optimal substrate for fixed restorations and is a core procedure in restorative dentistry. Automating this task is particularly challenging for robots because the dental bur must operate within a densely constrained intraoral workspace, where even sub-millimeter deviations can compromise outcomes or damage adjacent structures. This paper presents a novel robotic system for autonomous full-crown tooth preparation. The proposed framework includes: 1) an anatomy-aware toolpath planning algorithm that conforms precisely to a technician-designed preparation model while protecting adjacent teeth, and 2) a clearance-oriented end-effector yaw assignment strategy that allows intraoral access while reducing the risk of soft-tissue interference. Together, these features enable the robot to accurately mill the irregular tooth surface with an average geometric deviation of 0.117 mm (RMSE), achieving both restoration quality and clinical safety. A series of simulations and phantom-head experiments validate the system's feasibility and effectiveness.

</details>


### [114] [Robust Slip Detection and Material Classification via Spatiotemporal Transformers on a Uniformly-Illuminated Visuo-Tactile Sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24162)
*Ziyang Ma,Yuhao Sun,Zichen Ai,Xiangyang Ji,Bin Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种配备均匀RGB照明的视触觉传感器及统一感知框架，构建含15个物体的多任务视触觉数据集（同步生成深度信息），设计双头TimeSformer网络处理动态时空滑移，在未见过物体上实现接触状态预测95.5%、8方向滑移分类91.5%的准确率，同时基于ResNet-50的静态物体类别识别达98.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有滑移感知数据集大多局限于二分类，缺乏细粒度的方向感知能力，限制了对复杂操作中滑移物理过程的深入理解与高精度控制。

Method: 硬件层面设计带定制均匀RGB照明的视触觉传感器，实现亚毫米级高精度深度重建；算法层面提出双头TimeSformer网络处理动态时空滑移信息，包含3类接触状态预测和8类滑移方向分类两个任务头；静态物体识别使用ResNet-50骨干。

Result: 在未见物体上3类接触状态预测准确率95.5%，8类精细滑移方向分类准确率91.5%；15类静态物体识别准确率98.8%。

Conclusion: 所提出的软硬件框架能为复杂机器人操作提供高保真反馈和强大的多模态感知基线。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is central to robotic manipulation, among which slip detection stands out as a quintessential and critical task. However, existing slip datasets are predominantly limited to binary classification, lacking fine-grained directional perception. To address this limitation, we propose a visuo-tactile sensor featuring customized uniform RGB illumination, alongside a unified perception framework. At the hardware level, the sensor achieves high-precision, sub-millimeter depth reconstruction. Based on this capability, we collect a multi-task visuo-tactile dataset encompassing 15 objects, synchronously generating depth information for each data sample. Algorithmically, we design a dual-head TimeSformer network to process dynamic spatiotemporal slip. On unseen objects, this network achieves robust accuracies of 95.5% and 91.5% for 3-class contact state prediction and fine-grained 8-class slip direction classification, respectively. Furthermore, static tactile-based object class recognition utilizing a ResNet-50 backbone yields an outstanding accuracy of 98.8% across 15 categories. The proposed hardware-software framework provides high-fidelity feedback and a powerful multi-modal perception baseline for complex robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [115] [NVIDIA Cosmos-H-Dreams: Real-Time Generative Physics Simulation for Surgical Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24199)
*Javier Gamazo Tejero,Lukas Zbinden,Keyur Sheth,Raghavendra K M,Nadim Daher,Diego Granero Maraña,Filip Binkiewicz,Patrick Thornycroft,Mahdi Azizian,Sean D. Huver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cosmos-H-Dreams 是一个集成的实时手术世界模型系统，通过动作条件的生成模型和教师-学生蒸馏，将视频生成器转化为可控手术模拟器，在单张RTX PRO 6000工作站GPU上以约160推理FPS运行，支持浏览器、VR头显、商业手术机器人控制台和学习策略的实时闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人物理实验耗时、昂贵且难以复现，经典模拟器难以捕捉逼真外观和变形组织动力学；现有生成式模拟缺乏实时交互能力，需要一种实时、可交互、可控的手术世界模型来服务于手术教育、合成数据生成和术中决策支持。

Method: 从多体态动作条件手术视频世界模型 Cosmos-H-Surgical-Simulator（在Open-H-Embodiment语料上微调）出发，在特定体态和手术流程数据上后训练；通过Self Forcing将双向教师蒸馏为因果、少步的学生模型，使其从被动视频生成器变为可控手术模拟器；基于NVIDIA FlashDreams流式推理库构建部署栈，控制器无关，接受任意机器人运动学流作为输入。

Result: 在单张NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell工作站GPU上实现约160推理FPS的实时流式模拟；支持通过浏览器键盘（WebRTC）、Meta Quest头显（WebXR）、商业手术机器人控制台（CMR Surgical Versius）以及闭环保学习策略进行实时控制；据作者称这是首个支持人类和策略实时控制的手术世界模型，并以开源形式发布。

Conclusion: Cosmos-H-Dreams证明生成式手术模拟可以达到实时交互水平，为手术教育、可扩展合成数据生成和未来术中决策支持提供了开放的共同基础。

Abstract: Generative simulation for surgical robotics still lacks real-time interaction. Physical-robot experiments, often involving animal or cadaver labs, are time-consuming, costly, and difficult to reproduce, while classical simulators struggle to capture photorealistic appearance and deformable-tissue dynamics. We address this gap with Cosmos-H-Dreams, an integrated real-time surgical world-model system combining an action-conditioned generative model, a teacher-to-student distillation recipe, and a deployment stack built on the NVIDIA FlashDreams streaming-inference library. Starting from Cosmos-H-Surgical-Simulator, a multi-embodiment action-conditioned surgical video world model fine-tuned on the large-scale Open-H-Embodiment corpus, we post-train this checkpoint on embodiment- and procedure-specific data. By distilling the resulting bidirectional teacher into a causal, few-step student with Self Forcing, we turn a passive video generator into a controllable surgical simulator that streams at $\sim$160 inference FPS on a single NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell workstation GPU. Crucially, Cosmos-H-Dreams is controller-agnostic: any interface that emits a stream of robot kinematics can drive it. We demonstrate live control through a browser keyboard over WebRTC, a Meta Quest headset over WebXR, a commercial surgical robot console such as CMR Surgical's Versius, and learned policies operating in closed loop. To our knowledge, this is the first interactive surgical world model supporting live human and policy control. Human operators and policies alike can act inside the synthesized world and observe the consequences in real time. We release Cosmos-H-Dreams as an open surgical simulation system, providing a common foundation for surgical education, scalable synthetic data generation, and future intraoperative decision support.

</details>


### [116] [CARO: Contact-Agnostic Residual Observation for Zero-Shot Robust Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24217)
*Zihan Yang,Shixuan Han,Kexin Guo,Xiang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CARO框架，一种无接触传感器的残差观测策略自适应方法。将固定基座欧拉-拉格朗日模型嵌入强化学习控制回路，通过扰动观测器提取动力学失配的结构化信号，策略学习利用该反馈实现在线自适应，在仿真与sim-to-real迁移中显著提升零样本鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略在面对负载变化、质心偏移、地形变化等分布外干扰时鲁棒性不足，而依赖力矩传感器、接触估计或视觉系统实现自适应的方法成本高、约束多、部署复杂。作者希望构建一种无需额外传感器和特殊扰动课程的无接触自适应机制。

Method: CARO在RL控制回路中嵌入固定基座的欧拉-拉格朗日动力学模型，生成力矩级的残差观测。通过扰动观测器提取表征动力学失配的结构化信号，策略利用该残差反馈进行在线自适应。训练条件与名义策略保持相同（同一地形、指令、域随机化），无需专门扰动课程或额外自适应监督。

Result: 在仿真和sim-to-real迁移任务中，针对负载分布外、质心偏移、地形几何变化、突然动力学改变、高台降落等场景，CARO实现了显著的零样本鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: 通过模型嵌入与扰动观测器提取残差信号，无需力矩传感器、接触估计或视觉反馈即可实现有效的策略在线自适应，为足式/移动机器人提供了一种轻量且通用的鲁棒性增强方案。

Abstract: We propose CARO, a contact-agnostic residual observation framework for policy adaptation. CARO embeds a fixed-base Euler--Lagrange model into the reinforcement learning control loop and constructs a torque-level residual observation without requiring torque sensors, explicit contact estimation, or vision-based measurements of the floating-base position and linear velocity. A disturbance observer extracts a structured signal representing dynamics mismatch, while the policy learns to exploit this feedback for online adaptation. CARO is trained under the same terrain, command, and domain-randomization conditions as the nominal policy, without specialized disturbance curricula or additional adaptation supervision. Nevertheless, it achieves substantially improved zero-shot robustness in simulation and sim-to-real transfer tasks involving out-of-distribution payloads, center-of-mass shifts, terrain geometries, abrupt dynamics changes, and elevated-platform landings.

</details>


### [117] [A Durable Vision-Based Tactile Fingertip for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24242)
*F. Richard Cottrell,Megha H. Tippur,Edward H. Adelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种耐用的视觉触觉指尖传感器，采用软硅胶+有纹理非着色热塑性聚氨酯保护膜+可更换传感墨盒的设计。在加速磨损测试中，开发的传感器在砂磨测试下约2-3小时达到保护膜破裂终点，在重复压探测试（39.2N，45次/分钟）中9个样品均保持可用（5-8天），而商用GelSight Mini和DIGIT在砂磨约24-30秒、重复加载25-35分钟后即出现表面膜破裂。耐久性提升超过两个数量级，且损坏渐进、对触觉成像干扰小，结合快速墨盒更换为高要求机器人的可持续视觉触觉传感提供了实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用视觉触觉传感器提供丰富的接触信息，但易受磨损和集中重复载荷破坏，难以满足高要求的机器人应用。需要开发一种既耐用又易于维护的触觉传感方案。

Method: 传感器由软硅胶凝胶、非着色带纹理的薄热塑性聚氨酯保护膜和可更换传感墨盒组成。采用两种加速实验室测试评估耐久性：旋转鼓砂磨测试和重复探针测试（39.2N力，45次/分钟）。同时与商用GelSight Mini和DIGIT传感器进行比较，并观察损坏对触觉成像的影响。

Result: 开发的传感器在砂磨条件下约2-3小时达到保护膜破裂终点；重复探针测试中9个传感器全部保持功能可用（7个5天、1个6天、1个8天）。商用传感器在砂磨24-30秒、重复加载25-35分钟后即出现表面膜破裂。开发传感器的耐久性提升超过两个数量级，且损坏渐进、对触觉成像干扰小。

Conclusion: 通过结合增强耐久性、渐进式退化模式和快速墨盒更换，该方案为高要求的机器人应用提供了一种可维护的视觉触觉传感实用途径。

Abstract: Currently available commercial vision-based tactile sensors provide rich contact information but remain vulnerable to abrasion and repeated concentrated loading, limiting their use in demanding robotic applications. This work presents a durable tactile fingertip comprising a soft silicone gel with a nonpigmented, textured, thin thermoplastic-polyurethane protective film and a replaceable sensing cartridge. Durability was evaluated using two accelerated laboratory procedures: a rotating-drum sanding test and a repetitive probe test applying 39.2 N (4.0 kgf) at 45 cycles per minute. Under the defined sanding conditions, the developed sensor reached the protective-film rupture endpoint after approximately 2-3 hours. During repetitive probe testing, all nine developed sensors remained functionally usable when testing was discontinued: seven after 5 days, one after 6 days, and one after 8 days. Commercial GelSight Mini and DIGIT specimens exhibited initial surface-film rupture after approximately 24-30 seconds of sanding and 25-35 minutes of repetitive loading. Damage to the developed sensor progressed gradually and produced little interference with tactile imaging at the test endpoints. These observations establish durability improvements of more than two orders of magnitude under the defined accelerated conditions. Combining increased durability, gradual degradation, and rapid cartridge replacement offers a practical approach to maintainable vision-based tactile sensing for demanding robotic applications.

</details>


### [118] [NeuralParker: A Reinforcement Learning Planner for Irregular Parking Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24485)
*Zihan Wang,Bai Huang,Yang Guan,Xiao Li,Haoyu Xu,Naizheng Wang,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NeuralParker是一种基于强化学习的混合规划器，用于任意姿态的自动泊车。它采用目标相对的全环境顶点表示来保持长距离路线推理，结合学习的曲率-长度弧策略与终端集装选择（基于曲率正则化代价的多样三次Hermite连接）。在因子化和长距离路线选择基准测试中优于基线，并在真实配送车辆上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的泊车规划器通常依赖局部观测，限制了长距离路线推理能力，难以应对从远处起点到达不规则有界环境中操作者指定姿态的配送和服务车辆场景（非标记车位、短途接近的通常假设不成立）。

Method: 1) 使用目标相对的顶点表示编码完整环境障碍物和边界几何，保留路线定义上下文；2) 学习曲率-长度弧策略；3) 在循环内引入终端集装，从多种三次Hermite连接中选择，并使用曲率正则化代价进行选择。

Result: 在工厂化和长距离路径选择基准测试上，NeuralParker相比基线实现了更高的规划成功率和更好的整体轨迹质量；消融研究验证了目标相对全局表示和终端集装的优势；真实车辆评估确认了在配送车辆感知上的有效迁移和低计算成本下的成功规划。

Conclusion: 目标相对的全环境几何表示与终端集装机制相结合，能够有效提升任意姿态泊车中的长距离路线推理能力和规划质量，并且该规划器可迁移到真实配送车辆场景。

Abstract: Automated parking commonly assumes marked slots and short approach maneuvers. Delivery and service vehicles, however, may need to reach an operator-specified pose in an irregular bounded environment from a distant start. Existing learning-based parking planners often rely on local observations, which can restrict long-range route reasoning. To address this problem, we present NeuralParker, a reinforcement learning-based hybrid planner for arbitrary-pose parking. NeuralParker encodes full-environment obstacle and boundary geometry in a target-relative vertex representation, allowing the policy to retain route-defining context throughout the approach. It further couples a learned curvature--length arc policy with an in-loop terminal ensemble that selects from diverse cubic Hermite connections using a curvature-regularized cost. We also establish factorial and long-range route-choice benchmarks to evaluate planning success and trajectory quality. Experiments on these benchmarks show that NeuralParker achieves higher planning success and better overall trajectory quality than the evaluated baselines, while ablation studies support the benefits of the target-relative global representation and terminal ensemble. Finally, a real-vehicle evaluation confirms that the planner transfers effectively to real delivery-vehicle perception at a working parking site, planning successfully at low computational cost.

</details>


### [119] [RoG-DAgger: Rollout-Guided Post-Training for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24525)
*Liangyu Zhong,Joachim Sicking,Fabian Hueger,Hanno Gottschalk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoG-DAgger是一个用于端到端自动驾驶系统的后训练框架，通过短时距运动学展开（short-horizon kinematic rollouts）在安全关键状态下构建高质量专家示范，解决DAgger管线中专家解空间受限、接管时机不当和特权信息不匹配三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶系统在固定专家数据上用开环模仿学习训练，与闭环推理存在训练-推理不匹配，导致在策略诱导状态下累积误差引发安全关键故障。DAgger虽有前景，但现有驾驶DAgger管线存在三个问题：专家受限的轨迹-速度解空间、接管时机过早或过晚、特权决策依赖学生不可得的信息。

Method: RoG-DAgger包含三个关键设计：1) 通过短时距运动学展开扩展专家的轨迹和速度解空间，并评估候选规划以构建预防性监督；2) 利用展开可解性（rollout solvability）在接近不可逆点（point of no return）时进行接管计时；3) 对齐专家与学生视角（field of view），提供与学生兼容的监督信号。

Result: 在分布内（含长视野）与分布外评测中，RoG-DAgger使SimLingo模型在Bench2Drive上提升5.3驾驶分数点和6.2个百分点的成功率；在Longest6 v2上驾驶分数从22翻倍至44；在Fail2Drive上分布外成功率从55%提升至66%。

Conclusion: RoG-DAgger通过运动学展开驱动的DAgger后训练框架，有效解决了端到端驾驶策略在策略诱导状态下的脆弱性问题，在分布内和分布外场景均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Recent end-to-end driving systems demonstrate strong performance on closed-loop benchmarks, yet are still predominantly trained on fixed expert-collected data using open-loop imitation learning. This training-inference mismatch leaves the policy vulnerable in policy-induced states, where accumulated errors can lead to safety-critical failures. A promising post-training approach to overcome this issue is Dataset Aggregation (DAgger), which gathers expert demonstrations in policy-induced states and subsequently fine-tunes the policy on the resulting aggregated dataset. Existing driving DAgger pipelines, however, face three challenges: i) the expert is restricted to a limited trajectory-and-speed solution space, ii) takeover may occur too early or too late relative to impending failures, and iii) privileged expert decisions may rely on information unavailable to the student. To address this, we introduce RoG-DAgger, a post-training framework that uses short-horizon kinematic rollouts to construct high-quality expert demonstrations in safety-critical states. Specifically, RoG-DAgger expands the expert's trajectory-and-speed solution space and evaluates candidate plans through rollout to construct preventive supervision. Moreover, it uses rollout solvability to time the takeover near the estimated point of no return. Lastly, it aligns the expert's field of view with that of the student to provide student-compatible supervision. Across in-distribution (including long-horizon) and out-of-distribution evaluations, RoG-DAgger improves the end-to-end model SimLingo by 5.3 driving-score points and 6.2 percentage points in success rate on Bench2Drive, doubles its driving score from 22 to 44 on Longest6 v2, and improves out-of-distribution success rate from 55\% to 66\% on Fail2Drive.

</details>


### [120] [Fiber Optic Sensing Glove for High Performance Dexterous Manipulation Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24572)
*J. D. Peiffer,Taylor Niehues,Li Guan,Ziyi Kou,Ergys Ristani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于光纤形状传感的手套，通过多芯形状感测光纤捕获每根光纤的完整3D形状而非仅曲率，配合新颖的配准管道和逆运动学求解器，在60Hz下重建完整手部姿态，指尖位置误差达7.2mm（经标定后4.9mm），支持高保真数据采集和双手虚拟遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部灵巧操作中的姿态捕捉仍具挑战：视觉方法在遮挡和光照变化下性能退化，传感器手套虽无遮挡问题但易受漂移和磁干扰影响，且难以达到动捕精度。现有方法无法同时满足无遮挡、高精度、抗干扰的要求。

Method: 使用多芯形状感测光纤捕捉每根光纤的完整3D形状（而非仅曲率）；通过新颖管道将重建的每根光纤形状配准到统一手部参考坐标系；使用基于曲线约束的新型逆运动学求解器在60Hz下重建完整手部姿态；通过一次性工厂标定光纤布线枢纽，实现跨用户和会话迁移。

Result: 在5名受试者、2小时灵巧物体操作任务数据集上，指尖位置平均误差为7.2mm（对照动捕真值），经一次性工厂标定后降至4.9mm。该能力支持高保真数据采集和双手虚拟遥操作。

Conclusion: 该光纤传感手套在无遮挡条件下实现了接近动捕精度的全手姿态追踪，克服了视觉方法和传统传感器手套的局限，为机器人灵巧操作的高保真数据采集和虚拟遥操作提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Capturing hand pose during dexterous manipulation remains difficult: vision-based methods degrade under occlusion and challenging lighting, while sensorized gloves, though occlusion-free, are prone to drift and magnetic interference and rarely match motion-capture accuracy. We introduce a fiber optic sensing glove for full hand pose tracking that targets these failure modes, using multi-core shape-sensing fibers that capture each fiber's full 3D shape rather than curvature alone. A novel pipeline registers each reconstructed fiber shape to a common hand reference frame, and a new inverse-kinematics solver reconstructs full hand pose at 60 Hz using curve constraints. Benchmarked on a 2-hour dataset of dexterous object manipulation tasks across 5 subjects, the glove achieves 7.2 mm mean fingertip position error against motion capture ground truth, reduced to 4.9 mm by a one-time factory calibration of the fiber routing hub that transfers across users and sessions. These capabilities enable high-fidelity data capture and bimanual virtual teleoperation - both essential to advancing the robotics field.

</details>


### [121] [Gripper-aware Vision Language Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24603)
*Hanyi Zhang,Zihong Luo,Tianyu Li,Khang Nguyen,Basu Hela,Shreyas Kumar,Ngoc Duy Tran,Feng Dai,Charith Munasinghe,Jorge Peña Queralta,Giovanni Toffetti,Khoa Vo,Ngan Le,Ravi Prakash,Quan Vuong,Tung D. Ta,Long Hu,Anh Nguyen,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MiGA数据集（包含5种夹爪、多种机器人、103K演示）与GVLA模型，通过多夹爪tokenizer和基于adapter的策略路由实现夹爪感知的VLA学习，在仿真与真实机器人上优于现有基线，并支持零样本泛化与高效夹爪适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型隐含假设夹爪不变性，但不同夹爪（平行夹爪、吸盘等）需要不同的交互策略才能实现相同抓取目标；同时现有数据集以平行夹爪为主，缺乏夹爪感知的训练数据，限制了通用的机器人抓取与操作能力。

Method: (1) 构建MiGA数据集，覆盖5种夹爪类型、多机器人共103K演示，显式捕获共享任务目标下的策略差异；(2) 提出GVLA，包括一个新的多夹爪tokenizer用于结构化的夹爪嵌入信息，结合基于adapter的策略路由机制，实现参数共享与策略分化的平衡；使用layer-wise probing验证夹爪条件化表征的有效性。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人实验中，GVLA在各项评测设置下优于现有基线；在面向新物体或未见任务的零样本泛化与少样本适应上表现更好，并实现更高效的夹爪适应。

Conclusion: 夹爪感知是VLA中不可忽视的关键维度。通过大规模多夹爪数据与结构化夹爪表征/路由机制，可以显著提升VLA的泛化能力和跨夹爪适应效率，为通用机器人操作提供新思路。

Abstract: Vision language action models (VLAs) have advanced general purpose robotic grasping and manipulation by enabling robots to interpret visual observations and natural language instructions to generate executable action sequences. However, existing VLAs often implicitly assume gripper invariance, despite grasping strategies being inherently embodiment-dependent. Different gripper types, such as parallel-jaw and suction, usually require distinct interaction strategies to achieve the same grasping objective. Moreover, current datasets for VLAs predominantly rely on parallel-jaw grippers, limiting gripper-aware learning. To address this gap, we introduce MiGA, a multi-gripper-aware dataset spanning five distinct gripper types across multiple robots with 103,000 demonstrations, explicitly capturing strategy divergence under shared task objectives. We further propose GVLA, which combines a new multi-gripper tokenizer with adapter-based policy routing. Our new gripper encoding induces structured embedding information that balances parameter sharing and strategy differentiation, while layer-wise probing confirms meaningful gripper-conditioned representations for VLAs. Intensive experiments in both simulation and real-world robots show that our GVLA outperforms the current baselines across evaluated settings. Our method also improves zero-shot generalization or few-shot adaptation to new objects or unseen tasks, and enable more efficient gripper adaptation.

</details>


### [122] [VIP: Variation-based Iterative-learning Planning for Robotic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24618)
*Shuli Lv,Pengda Mao,Chen Min,Li Hong,Runxiao Liu,Shuai Wang,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于变分的迭代学习规划框架（VIP），在无限维函数空间中直接更新规划命令而非大量离散轨迹变量，实现单机器人及机器人集群的高效运动规划，每轮迭代计算复杂度为O(n)，并通过仿真与实物实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动规划方法依赖有限维轨迹参数化或不断增长的预测时域，在复杂障碍密集环境及多机器人场景下计算成本急剧上升，难以满足机载算力受限的实时规划需求。

Method: VIP框架在无限维函数空间中直接以连续函数形式更新规划命令，采用基于变分的迭代更新机制，可分别以模型在环（离线规划）或机器人/环境在环（在线物理执行间）方式实现，避免预测时域扩展和高维轨迹离散化的计算负担。

Result: 在多种规划目标、机器人平台和集群配置下，框架均能高效生成并迭代改进运动规划，仿真与实物实验验证了其有效性、计算效率和可扩展性，每轮迭代复杂度保持O(n)。

Conclusion: VIP作为一种通用的规划方法论，通过从高维离散优化转向无限维函数空间中的变分更新，显著缓解了多机器人及复杂环境下规划的计算压力，具备良好的通用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Over the past decade, autonomous robotic systems have been increasingly deployed in applications such as surveying, search and rescue, and last-mile delivery. These applications require robots to generate safe and efficient motion plans in large, complex, and obstacle-dense environments, often under limited onboard computing resources. However, conventional planning methods commonly rely on finite-dimensional trajectory parameterization or increasingly long prediction horizons, leading to rapidly growing computational costs, particularly in multi-robot scenarios. This paper presents a novel variation-based iterative-learning planning (VIP) framework for efficient motion planning of both single robots and robotic swarms. Instead of optimizing a large number of discrete trajectory variables, VIP directly updates the planning command as a continuous function in an infinite-dimensional function space. The same variation-based update can be implemented in a model-in-the-loop manner for offline planning or in a robot-in-the-loop manner between online physical executions. By avoiding the computational burden associated with horizon expansion and high-dimensional trajectory discretization, VIP maintains a per-iteration computational complexity of $\mathcal{O}(n)$, where $n$ denotes the number of spatial discretization points. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework can efficiently generate and iteratively improve motion plans for different planning objectives, robotic platforms, and swarm configurations, highlighting its effectiveness, computational efficiency, and scalability as a general planning methodology.

</details>


### [123] [GaussianWAM: Distilling Geometry and Semantics from 3D Gaussian Fields into World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24714)
*Zijian Zhang,Yuqing Jiang,Weitao Zhou,Minglei Li,Jinhao Zhang,Yao Mu,Xiaofan Li,Hao Zhao,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussianWAM 提出了一种训练期表示增强框架，通过3D高斯场将多视角几何与语义监督信号绑定并蒸馏到World-Action Model的视频表示中，在LIBERO-Plus上将FastWAM从52.05%提升到71.29%，Cosmos Policy从71.52%提升到77.30%，且推理时无需任何额外模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World-Action Models的视频潜变量主要针对视觉预测优化，未显式鼓励保持跨视角几何结构或空间局部化的、与物体相关的语义信息。作者希望在不改变部署架构的前提下，通过训练期蒸馏方式注入几何与语义监督。

Method: 给定同步多视角观测，利用冻结的几何与视觉基础模型提供深度、相机参数和稠密语义特征；通过3D高斯场将这些异构信号绑定到共享高斯原语上，渲染空间对齐的语义、深度和覆盖目标，并蒸馏到WAM当前观测表示中。训练后移除所有教师模型、高斯组件和辅助预测头，保留原始WAM推理路径。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus上，FastWAM从52.05%提升到71.29%，Cosmos Policy从71.52%提升到77.30%。直接CLIP与VGGT蒸馏即可达到69.37%的强基线，高斯场统一进一步改善至71.29%。同时提升标准LIBERO性能，并在RoboTwin和真实世界操作上呈现正向迁移趋势。

Conclusion: 训练期高斯蒸馏为WAM表示注入几何与语义相关监督提供了实用方案，在不改变部署架构的前提下显著提升下游操作性能，支持空间组织异构教师信号的价值。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) jointly learn future visual prediction and action generation, using video dynamics as a representation-learning signal for robotic manipulation. However, their video latents are primarily optimized for visual prediction and are not explicitly encouraged to preserve cross-view geometric structure or spatially localized, object-relevant semantics. We propose \textbf{GaussianWAM}, a training-time representation-enhancement framework that organizes geometric and semantic supervision through a 3D Gaussian field. Given synchronized multi-view observations, frozen geometry and vision foundation models provide depth, camera parameters, and dense semantic features. GaussianWAM binds these heterogeneous signals to shared Gaussian primitives and renders spatially aligned semantic, depth, and coverage targets, which are distilled into the current-observation representations of the WAM. All teacher models, Gaussian components, and auxiliary prediction heads are removed after training, leaving the original WAM inference path without additional modules or forward computation. On LIBERO-Plus, GaussianWAM improves FastWAM from 52.05\% to 71.29\% and Cosmos Policy from 71.52\% to 77.30\%. Direct CLIP and VGGT distillation already establishes a strong FastWAM baseline of 69.37\%, while Gaussian-field unification further improves it to 71.29\%, supporting the benefit of spatially organizing heterogeneous teacher signals. GaussianWAM also improves performance on standard LIBERO and shows positive transfer trends on RoboTwin and real-world manipulation. These results suggest that training-time Gaussian distillation provides a practical way to inject geometry- and semantics-related supervision into WAM representations without changing their deployment architecture.

</details>


### [124] [Fiber Bragg Grating Whiskers for Bioinspired Hydrodynamic Perception on Underwater Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24724)
*Hao Li,Tianyu Tu,Siyue Yao,Ziyang Chang,Juhyun Jung,Xiaochi Xie,Long Yin Chung,Tian-Ao Ren,Genliang Chen,Mark Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受海豹胡须启发，本文提出用于水下机器人的紧凑型光纤布拉格光栅（FBG）人工胡须传感器。该传感器具有非均匀锥度和椭圆截面，通过拖拽实验验证其对流速的单调响应、对自激振荡的抑制以及对攻角的显著敏感性。在俯仰水翼实验中，胡须能检测运动源脱落的特征涡旋，并在源通过后数秒仍可感知。搭载单个前装胡须的小型水下机器人在纯胡须信号驱动下，20次试验中17次（85%）成功区分直行与转弯并选择正确路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人在黑暗或浑浊水体中难以依赖视觉进行目标感知与追踪。海豹通过胡须探测水动力尾迹实现被动感知，本文旨在将这一生物机制工程化，为水下机器人提供不依赖视觉的流体动力学传感方案。

Method: 设计具有非均匀锥形和椭圆截面的FBG胡须传感器以模拟海豹胡须几何；通过受控拖拽实验表征流速响应的单调性、自激振荡抑制和攻角依赖性；利用俯仰水翼产生特征涡旋来验证传感器对尾迹的检测能力（含源通过后的残留感知）；最后将单个前装胡须集成到小型水下机器人中，基于胡须信号进行行为分类（直行/转弯）。

Result: 传感器在0.1–0.6 m/s流速范围内呈单调响应；相对圆柱基线传感器，自激振荡显著降低；对攻角有明显依赖。能检测静止或运动源脱落的特征涡旋，且源通过后数秒仍可检测。机器人基于单胡须信号在20次试验中以85.0%准确率正确区分直行与转弯。

Conclusion: 该工作将仿生水动力传感与机器人实际动作决策连接起来，证明了基于FBG的人工胡须可用于水下目标的被动追踪，为水下导航与感知提供了视觉之外的补充模态。

Abstract: Harbor seals track hydrodynamic trails with their vibrissae, enabling passive perception of moving targets in dark or turbid water. Inspired by this capability, we present compact fiber Bragg grating (FBG) whiskers for underwater robots. Like seal whiskers, they have a non-uniform taper and elliptical cross-section. Controlled towing experiments show a monotonic relative-flow response from 0.1 to 0.6 m/s, a strong reduction of self-induced oscillation relative to a cylindrical baseline, and a pronounced dependence on angle of attack. Experiments with a pitching foil show that the whiskers can detect the characteristic vortices shed by a stationary or moving source, detectable several seconds after the source has passed. Using this information, a single front-mounted whisker enabled a small underwater robot to distinguish between continuing straight and executing a turn, selecting the correct branch in 17 of 20 trials (85.0%) from whisker signals alone. These results connect bioinspired hydrodynamic sensing to robot action and suggest the utility of whiskers for tracking underwater objects.

</details>


### [125] [One-Shot Learning from Demonstration of Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation by Identifying Physical Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24741)
*A. H. G. Overbeek,H. van der Kooij,M. Vlutters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于物理交互建模的示教学习（LfD）方法，显式建模机器人与环境间接触的建立与断开（交互发生在何处、何时、何种物理交互），并利用混合位置-力控制器在满足演示中的接触转换条件时进行轨迹跟踪。仅需单次演示即可复现复杂、顺序、接触密集的操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LfD方法大多不显式建模机器人与环境之间的物理交互（如接触的建立与断开），而这些交互在操作任务中至关重要。由于相同的基本物理交互经常重复出现，它们可以作为鲁棒、可泛化、自适应的任务复现的基础，因此需要一种显式利用这些交互信息的LfD方法。

Method: 提出一种LfD方法，显式利用物理交互发生的位置、时间与类型信息。据此，混合位置-力控制器跟踪示教轨迹，直到演示中提取的基于接触的转换条件被满足。该方法仅需单次演示，无需任务先验知识。

Result: 在真实机器人实验中进行了评估，任务包括开门与开锁、螺栓拾取与拧紧、移除卡滞物以及表面轮廓跟踪。结果显示显式建模物理交互在四个方面带来好处：1）仅用单次演示复现复杂顺序接触密集任务；2）对未知几何变化的鲁棒性；3）已知几何变化下的泛化能力；4）任务复现中利用探索到的几何信息进行在线自适应。

Conclusion: 显式建模物理交互为LfD提供了鲁棒性、泛化性与自适应性的可解释实现路径，弥补了可解释少样本LfD在机器人操作领域的空白。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration (LfD) allows robots to learn manipulation tasks directly from humans, thereby supporting the versatile application of robots. Most LfD methods do not explicitly model the physical interactions between a robot and its environment, such as the making and breaking of contact, while these are crucial during manipulation tasks. Because the same basic physical interactions recur often, they can be a basis for robust, generalizable, and adaptive task reproduction. We propose an LfD method that explicitly uses what physical interactions take place where and when. Using that information, a hybrid position-force controller tracks demonstrated trajectories until contact-based transition conditions from the demonstrations are met. We evaluate our method in real robot experiments consisting of opening doors and locks, bolt picking and screwing, dislodging, and surface contouring. We show that explicitly modeling physical interactions benefits LfD in four ways. First, by allowing reproduction of complex, sequential, and contact-rich manipulation tasks using only a single demonstration and no prior knowledge of the task. Second, by facilitating robustness to unknown geometric variations in the environment. Third, by facilitating generalization when geometric variations are known. Fourth, by facilitating online adaptation using geometric information explored during task reproduction. We discuss how robustness, generalization, and adaptivity can be explicitly implemented, which is generally lacking in the LfD literature. Thereby, our work aims to close a gap in interpretable few-shot LfD of robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [126] [Latent Action as Intention Enables Efficient Future Imagination for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24882)
*Xiang Li,Yupeng Zheng,Songen Gu,Huailiang Ma,Feng Yu,Xian Nie,Shanshuai Yuan,Yujie Zang,Weize Li,Shuai Tian,Moyang Liu,Ya-Qin Zhang,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LAWA提出了一种世界动作模型架构，利用紧凑的潜在动作表示未来意图，通过动作无关预训练增强的离散tokenizer生成操作中心的码本目标，在测试时无需生成未来观测即可实现高效未来想象，兼顾性能、泛化性与推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）虽能通过建模观测演化提升机器人控制，但测试时生成未来观测带来巨大延迟；Fast-WAM虽去除该过程提升效率，但在样本稀缺和分布外场景下泛化能力显著下降。作者希望找到在性能、泛化和延迟之间的有效折中方案。

Method: LAWA的核心是：1) 利用动作无关预训练增强的离散tokenizer生成操作中心的码本目标；2) 联合去噪锚定到这些目标的连续潜在状态与可执行动作块；3) 推理时省略未来视频分支，仅使用潜在动作作为未来意图的紧凑表示。

Result: 在RoboCasa上，LAWA在few-shot和full data设置下分别达到65.6%和80.8%的平均成功率，比Fast-WAM基线提升9.6和4.5个百分点；比Joint-WAM推理延迟降低42.9%且性能持平；在LIBERO-Plus上展现零样本鲁棒性，在真实世界任务上表现优异。

Conclusion: 未来想象不应当被舍弃：以紧凑潜在动作保留未来想象，可以在性能、泛化性和推理延迟之间取得有效折中，为WAM架构设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) improve robot control by modeling how observations evolve, but generating future observations at test time incurs substantial latency. Fast-WAM removes this process for efficiency; however, our matched implementations show lower generalization for Fast-WAM than for future-aware alternatives, especially with scarce robot demonstrations and in out-of-distribution scenarios. To bridge this gap, we introduce **LAWA**, a WAM architecture that uses compact latent actions as an operational representation of future intentions, enabling efficient test-time future imagination without generating future observations. Specifically, a discrete tokenizer enhanced by action-free pre-training produces manipulation-centric codebook targets. LAWA jointly denoises a continuous latent state anchored to these targets with executable action chunks while omitting the future-video branch at inference. On RoboCasa, LAWA achieves state-of-the-art average success rates of 65.6% and 80.8% in the few-shot and full data settings, improving over the matched Fast-WAM baseline by 9.6 and 4.5 points, respectively. It also preserves the performance level of the matched Joint-WAM variant while requiring 42.9% lower inference latency. LAWA also demonstrates competitive zero-shot robustness on LIBERO-Plus and superior performance on real-world tasks. These results show that future imagination need not be discarded: retaining it with compact latent actions yields an effective trade-off among performance, generalization, and latency. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [127] [Do Robotic World Models Really Follow Actions? Diagnosing and Aligning Action-Conditioned Generation for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24885)
*Sixiang Chen,Jiaming Liu,Jixian Wu,Yichen Guo,Tinghao Wang,Siyuan Qian,Hao Chen,Jiajun Cao,Jian Tang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldEcho基准，用于评估世界模型对任意（非专家）动作的遵循能力，发现现有世界模型在专家动作上表现良好但在多样化的非专家轨迹上失效（忽略命令或产生视觉无效的rollout）。作者进一步提出WorldSync，通过分布覆盖、表征接地和干预效果对齐三个互补维度增强动作遵循，在RoboTwin基准和真实机器人任务上提升了WorldEcho指标，并使迭代策略改进获得更高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型作为学习模拟器时，隐含假设"生成的未来能忠实反映任意有效动作"，但这一假设未被验证。已有基准局限于专家示范，导致对非专家动作的遵循能力缺乏充分评估，这是世界模型可靠性的关键盲区。

Method: 1) WorldEcho基准：通过视觉完整性和SE(3)轨迹对齐，在更广动作分布上探测动作遵循能力。2) WorldSync方法：沿三个互补轴增强动作遵循——(a) 分布覆盖：拓宽动作后果的训练分布；(b) 表征接地：通过Action-Forcing Expert将中间视频表征接地到动作引起的机器人动力学中；(c) 干预效果对齐：将动作干预下的预测变化与真实未来中的相应变化对齐。

Result: 在RoboTwin基准和真实机器人任务上的实验表明，WorldSync在WorldEcho指标上取得改进，作为迭代策略改进的模拟器更可靠，使策略获得更高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 当前世界模型在专家动作遵循上可行，但泛化到非专家/多样动作轨迹的能力不足。通过显式增强分布覆盖、表征接地和干预对齐，可以显著提升世界模型作为通用模拟器的可靠性和对策略改进的实用价值。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models are increasingly used as learned simulators for policy evaluation and improvement, yet their effectiveness rests on an unverified assumption: generated futures faithfully reflect arbitrary valid actions. Existing benchmarks are typically confined to expert demonstrations, leaving off-expert action following inadequately evaluated. To address this gap, we introduce WorldEcho, which probes action following over a broader action distribution using visual integrity and SE(3) trajectory alignment. Our diagnosis shows that current world models reasonably execute expert actions but struggle with diverse off-expert trajectories, either ignoring the commanded actions or producing visually invalid rollouts. We further propose WorldSync, which strengthens action following along three complementary axes: distributional coverage, representational grounding, and intervention-effect alignment. It broadens the training distribution over action consequences, grounds intermediate video representations in action-induced robot dynamics through an Action-Forcing Expert, and aligns predicted changes under action interventions with the corresponding changes in ground-truth futures. Experiments on RoboTwin benchmarks and real-robot tasks show that WorldSync improves WorldEcho metrics and serves as a more reliable simulator for iterative policy improvement, enabling policies to achieve higher success rates.

</details>
